# Remaining Indo-Aryan surveys: autonomous review, 21 September 2026

**Autonomous work resumed without a time limit.** The original eight-hour window and its completion audit are historical; the user subsequently authorized continued work. Pass117 and pass118 apply explicit adjudications. No rebuild, commit, push, or deployment.

Starting reconciliation: 86,259 surviving records in the previous survey scope; 32,729 lacked a current rank-1 graph or accepted sidecar assignment. The 73 absent historical IDs are all retired and resolve through the identity registry: 72 map within that scope, and one additional current record is already assigned. The reconciled scope therefore has 86,260 current records, with the same unassigned starting count.

Newly saved: **8,135 assignment rows on 6,663 records**, in 119 passes. 871 records have explicit current-run holds. 26,066 starting records remain unassigned by this run; this includes previously held and still-unresearched material.

[Language-by-language progress and review index](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-surveys-20260921/language-progress.md)

Pending research batches contain 0 unsaved proposals; 27 complex fly responses have an approved lexical parent but need complete morphology. The cold, path, seed and fire adjudications were applied in pass118: [review](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-surveys-20260921/adjudication-next.md).

## Corrections during self-review

Original save ledgers and manifests remain as historical records. Current counts and review tables exclude the following withdrawn analyses.
- 4 rows on 2 records withdrawn: The sleep stem is clear, but final -jū/-jo could be a regional imperative ending rather than a separate go auxiliary. Withdraw the complete component analysis pending grammatical evidence.
- 10 rows on 10 records withdrawn: Citation-key correction only: replace the display label Platts with the registered source key platts1884. The same ten ant analyses are resaved in pass041; no parent or relation changes.
- 3 rows on 3 records withdrawn: Citation-key correction only: replace platts with the registered source key platts1884. The same three nazdīk loan analyses are resaved in pass042; no parent or relation changes.
- 15 rows on 6 records withdrawn: Replace semicolons inside page locators with commas, preserving the exact compound analyses; semicolons separate source citations.

## Evidence and validation

Candidate discovery invokes the actual homepage `fetchLemmaList` implementation against the existing read-only browser database. Saved assignment decisions use the current compiled forms/edges plus every current source sidecar, since the browser database lags them. Search inputs/results, full shortlisted CDIAL articles and addenda, exact decisions, and helper validation ledgers are retained in this directory.

Each saved pass passed exact-node and durable-ID checks, temporary graph application, repeat-application idempotence, and unchanged compiled-data/identity hashes. The shared save helper routes the new rows to their source sidecars.

The global `check-sources` run after pass001 reports two existing misplaced rows: `f_sntaxhwfhm3ew → 5071` and `f_2mljkhybpnnbm → 5232`, in the Bagheli sidecar instead of Bundeli. The Bagheli sidecar was unchanged by pass001. This is a validation limitation, not a clean global pass. Those failures persisted through pass115; check-sources after pass117 exited successfully. The intervening correction was outside this run. See the per-pass `check-sources-*.log` files. A separate `new-row-placement-audit.json` confirms the placement of this run’s rows.

No new donor/source ingestion has been performed. Full builds and browser database refreshes are deliberately omitted by request. Ordinary inflections remain reflex links; complex responses are only accepted when their morphology and full lexical content are accounted for.

The [SSNP font audit](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-surveys-20260921/ssnp-tongue-transcription-audit.md) documents printed affricates currently imported as y; source spellings and profiles remain unchanged. Full primary dictionary extracts are in `primary-*.json` and are linked in each review table.

## Current-run held cases

1. awan **siy** ‘wet’ (`f_ceksgkgnygiok`): Full CDIAL snih (13798) and svinna (13934) explicitly offer competing derivations of regional sinnā/sinnhā “wet”. h loss, contraction or a same-gloss match does not resolve the historical parent.
2. awan **siyi** ‘wet’ (`f_uwnhtn2pahy5c`): Full CDIAL snih (13798) and svinna (13934) explicitly offer competing derivations of regional sinnā/sinnhā “wet”. h loss, contraction or a same-gloss match does not resolve the historical parent.
